        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Drop-to-Generation feature. When user drags an image or video file anywhere over the app, show a new small drop zone above the GenerationsPane's control tab (the pane handle). Dropping there uploads the file as a new generation. For files >=50MB, keep the file local via FileSystemHandle persisted in IndexedDB, and only upload when a worker or timeline agent actually needs the bytes — the gallery displays the local file via blob URL in the meantime. Milestones: (1) Global drag detection + chip above pane tab (UI only, no drop action yet). (2) <50MB path — drop uploads via existing image/video uploaders + creates a generation with no shot attachment. (3) >=50MB path — FileSystemHandle + IndexedDB persistence, local-mode generation rows, thumbnail-only upload. (4) Media resolution — gallery displays local-mode generations via blob URL, with permission re-grant UX. (5) Lazy upload at task-build time for local-mode generations, with storage_mode flip. (6) Fallback path for browsers without FileSystemHandle API (Firefox/Safari force upload with warning). Research context already gathered — see arguments passed to megaplan-skill for concrete paths/lines (GenerationsPane.tsx, PaneControlTab.tsx, panesStore.ts, externalImageDrop.ts, imageUploader.ts, videoUploader.ts, storagePaths.ts, MediaGallery/types.ts). Key design decisions to flag as questions: (a) split threshold images 25MB / videos 100MB vs unified 50MB; (b) project-scoped vs user-scoped local handles; (c) precedence vs inner tool drop zones (stopPropagation); (d) handle invalidation when source file moves/renames; (e) whether to always upload small downsized thumbnails for local-mode gallery display without handle permission. Risks to call out: FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle (prompt state across reloads), Chromium-only API coverage, blob URL lifetime in React, lazy-upload failure modes mid-task-build, concurrent worker consumption of same local handle.

    "Document-level `preventDefault` on dragover/drop (Files types only) MUST remain additive to feature-local drop zones (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts:431-508`, `src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useClipManager.ts:377-417`, shot-group droppables). Inner zones attach element-scoped listeners; both fire via bubbling \u2014 do not stopPropagation globally.",
    "FLAG-001: Both `.not('location', 'is', null)` filters in `useProjectGenerations.ts:264-268` AND :318-319 must be updated identically; otherwise count/data queries drift and pagination offsets skew.",
    "FLAG-002: Without the document-level preventDefault, a user dropping a file that misses every zone causes the browser to navigate away from the app. This must ship in Phase 1 before any local-handle work.",
    "FLAG-003: Client-side gates alone are insufficient \u2014 the server-side timeline agent path (`supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts:371-405`, `tools/generation.ts:384-411`) cannot see IndexedDB. Both the shot-attach pre-materialization AND the edge-fn guard must land.",
    "FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle: `queryPermission` only returns `granted|denied|prompt`; `requestPermission` MUST be called inside a user gesture. The resolver must never auto-request \u2014 always surface `needs-permission` and let the overlay trigger on click.",
    "Blob URL lifetime: register once per generation id in a module-level map, revoke on unmount or id change. Multiple gallery tiles sharing a generation must share the same URL or you leak.",
    "Single-flight in `materializeLocalGeneration` is load-bearing \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. Use a module-level `Map<generationId, Promise>`.",
    "Materialization failure modes (permission-denied, handle-missing, network) must each distinctly flip the row back to `local` and surface an actionable error code \u2014 never leave a row stuck in `storage_mode='uploading'`.",
    "Split threshold (25 MB images / 100 MB videos) is assumed. Export from `dropToGenerationConfig.ts` so it's a single-file tune knob. If the user picks unified 50 MB, this is still one-file change.",
    "Inner tool drop zones must continue working after the global listener lands \u2014 manually verify video-editor, join-clips, and shot-group drops in Phase 1 validation (T4).",
    "Generation transformer change relaxes `RawGeneration.location` to `string | null`. Every downstream consumer that indexes `.location` without null checks is a latent crash \u2014 grep `primary_variant?.location || item.location` for all call sites and audit them.",
    "Worker/edge pipeline must keep working for existing remote-mode generations. The migration is additive (default `'remote'`) but Phase 4 transformer/query changes must not regress remote-row rendering.",
    "After successful materialization, keep the IndexedDB handle AND the `local_media_handles` row per the approved assumption \u2014 zero disk cost, supports retry if remote copy is later lost.",
    "Video thumbnail helper: the plan mentions a ~30 LOC first-frame canvas capture if no existing helper is found. Before writing one, search `src/shared/media/` for existing helpers (`extractVideoPosterFrame`, `extractAndUploadThumbnailOnly`, `videoThumbnailGenerator.ts`) \u2014 reuse beats reimplementing.",
    "CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule: NO success toast on drop \u2014 only surface toasts for unsupported MIME, oversized-in-Phase-2, upload failure, and materialization failure.",
    "Edge fn deploy discipline: deploy `create-task` individually (`npx supabase functions deploy create-task --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc`) \u2014 never batch.",
    "DB migration discipline: `npx supabase db push --linked` \u2014 NEVER `db reset --linked`.",
    "Shot-attach pre-materialization makes 'attach a large local generation to a shot' a slow operation. The approved UX is a progress toast \u2014 do not silently hang; show per-file upload progress.",
    "Cross-browser fallback (Firefox/Safari) should gracefully degrade to full upload, never leave the user with a half-inserted generation or a crashed drop handler.",
    "Gallery list-view MUST use `thumbnail_url` (uploaded at drop time) \u2014 only the lightbox should open the local handle. This is how we avoid a permission prompt on every page load."

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: Phase 1 (T1-T4) MUST ship before any drop logic because the document-level `preventDefault` protects the rest of the feature from the 'drop misses every zone \u2192 browser navigates away' hazard. Phases 2\u21923\u21924\u21925\u21926 are sequential by data dependency (migration before query, query before resolver, resolver before materialization). Cross-phase: never let a commit land with typecheck failing \u2014 run `npm run typecheck` after each phase and after T13 in particular, since relaxing `RawGeneration.location` will surface latent null-unsafe consumers. The two highest-risk areas are (a) blob URL lifetime \u2014 memoize in a module-level registry keyed by generation id and revoke on unmount, and (b) `materializeLocalGeneration` single-flight \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. For the edge fn (T18), deploy individually per CLAUDE.md \u2014 never batch. For the migration (T8), `db push --linked` only, never `db reset`. CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule applies throughout: no success toasts on drop or materialize. Default assumptions to preserve unless the user overrides: split threshold (images 25 MB / videos 100 MB), user-scoped handles, inner zones win over chip, per-tile broken-handle state, thumbnails always uploaded at drop time, handles kept after materialization. If the user picks a different threshold, it's a one-file change in `dropToGenerationConfig.ts`. When searching for the URL-resolution chokepoint in T14, start by grepping `primary_variant?.location` and `item.location` \u2014 wire the resolver in a single place to avoid drift. When identifying entry points in T16, remember there are TWO client-side shot-attach mutations (`useAddImageToShot` and the `WithoutPosition` peer) \u2014 both need gates. Plan validation: all 22 approved plan steps map 1:1 to T1-T21 with T22 as the cross-cutting final verification. Step 16 (identify entry points) is folded into T16 per the 'no inspect tasks' rule \u2014 the identification work is the first step of T16's implementation.",

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Drop-to-Generation feature. When user drags an image or video file anywhere over the app, show a new small drop zone above the GenerationsPane's control tab (the pane handle). Dropping there uploads the file as a new generation. For files >=50MB, keep the file local via FileSystemHandle persisted in IndexedDB, and only upload when a worker or timeline agent actually needs the bytes — the gallery displays the local file via blob URL in the meantime. Milestones: (1) Global drag detection + chip above pane tab (UI only, no drop action yet). (2) <50MB path — drop uploads via existing image/video uploaders + creates a generation with no shot attachment. (3) >=50MB path — FileSystemHandle + IndexedDB persistence, local-mode generation rows, thumbnail-only upload. (4) Media resolution — gallery displays local-mode generations via blob URL, with permission re-grant UX. (5) Lazy upload at task-build time for local-mode generations, with storage_mode flip. (6) Fallback path for browsers without FileSystemHandle API (Firefox/Safari force upload with warning). Research context already gathered — see arguments passed to megaplan-skill for concrete paths/lines (GenerationsPane.tsx, PaneControlTab.tsx, panesStore.ts, externalImageDrop.ts, imageUploader.ts, videoUploader.ts, storagePaths.ts, MediaGallery/types.ts). Key design decisions to flag as questions: (a) split threshold images 25MB / videos 100MB vs unified 50MB; (b) project-scoped vs user-scoped local handles; (c) precedence vs inner tool drop zones (stopPropagation); (d) handle invalidation when source file moves/renames; (e) whether to always upload small downsized thumbnails for local-mode gallery display without handle permission. Risks to call out: FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle (prompt state across reloads), Chromium-only API coverage, blob URL lifetime in React, lazy-upload failure modes mid-task-build, concurrent worker consumption of same local handle.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: Phase 1 (T1-T4) MUST ship before any drop logic because the document-level `preventDefault` protects the rest of the feature from the 'drop misses every zone \u2192 browser navigates away' hazard. Phases 2\u21923\u21924\u21925\u21926 are sequential by data dependency (migration before query, query before resolver, resolver before materialization). Cross-phase: never let a commit land with typecheck failing \u2014 run `npm run typecheck` after each phase and after T13 in particular, since relaxing `RawGeneration.location` will surface latent null-unsafe consumers. The two highest-risk areas are (a) blob URL lifetime \u2014 memoize in a module-level registry keyed by generation id and revoke on unmount, and (b) `materializeLocalGeneration` single-flight \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. For the edge fn (T18), deploy individually per CLAUDE.md \u2014 never batch. For the migration (T8), `db push --linked` only, never `db reset`. CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule applies throughout: no success toasts on drop or materialize. Default assumptions to preserve unless the user overrides: split threshold (images 25 MB / videos 100 MB), user-scoped handles, inner zones win over chip, per-tile broken-handle state, thumbnails always uploaded at drop time, handles kept after materialization. If the user picks a different threshold, it's a one-file change in `dropToGenerationConfig.ts`. When searching for the URL-resolution chokepoint in T14, start by grepping `primary_variant?.location` and `item.location` \u2014 wire the resolver in a single place to avoid drift. When identifying entry points in T16, remember there are TWO client-side shot-attach mutations (`useAddImageToShot` and the `WithoutPosition` peer) \u2014 both need gates. Plan validation: all 22 approved plan steps map 1:1 to T1-T21 with T22 as the cross-cutting final verification. Step 16 (identify entry points) is folded into T16 per the 'no inspect tasks' rule \u2014 the identification work is the first step of T16's implementation.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Drop-to-Generation feature. When user drags an image or video file anywhere over the app, show a new small drop zone above the GenerationsPane's control tab (the pane handle). Dropping there uploads the file as a new generation. For files >=50MB, keep the file local via FileSystemHandle persisted in IndexedDB, and only upload when a worker or timeline agent actually needs the bytes — the gallery displays the local file via blob URL in the meantime. Milestones: (1) Global drag detection + chip above pane tab (UI only, no drop action yet). (2) <50MB path — drop uploads via existing image/video uploaders + creates a generation with no shot attachment. (3) >=50MB path — FileSystemHandle + IndexedDB persistence, local-mode generation rows, thumbnail-only upload. (4) Media resolution — gallery displays local-mode generations via blob URL, with permission re-grant UX. (5) Lazy upload at task-build time for local-mode generations, with storage_mode flip. (6) Fallback path for browsers without FileSystemHandle API (Firefox/Safari force upload with warning). Research context already gathered — see arguments passed to megaplan-skill for concrete paths/lines (GenerationsPane.tsx, PaneControlTab.tsx, panesStore.ts, externalImageDrop.ts, imageUploader.ts, videoUploader.ts, storagePaths.ts, MediaGallery/types.ts). Key design decisions to flag as questions: (a) split threshold images 25MB / videos 100MB vs unified 50MB; (b) project-scoped vs user-scoped local handles; (c) precedence vs inner tool drop zones (stopPropagation); (d) handle invalidation when source file moves/renames; (e) whether to always upload small downsized thumbnails for local-mode gallery display without handle permission. Risks to call out: FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle (prompt state across reloads), Chromium-only API coverage, blob URL lifetime in React, lazy-upload failure modes mid-task-build, concurrent worker consumption of same local handle.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: Phase 1 (T1-T4) MUST ship before any drop logic because the document-level `preventDefault` protects the rest of the feature from the 'drop misses every zone \u2192 browser navigates away' hazard. Phases 2\u21923\u21924\u21925\u21926 are sequential by data dependency (migration before query, query before resolver, resolver before materialization). Cross-phase: never let a commit land with typecheck failing \u2014 run `npm run typecheck` after each phase and after T13 in particular, since relaxing `RawGeneration.location` will surface latent null-unsafe consumers. The two highest-risk areas are (a) blob URL lifetime \u2014 memoize in a module-level registry keyed by generation id and revoke on unmount, and (b) `materializeLocalGeneration` single-flight \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. For the edge fn (T18), deploy individually per CLAUDE.md \u2014 never batch. For the migration (T8), `db push --linked` only, never `db reset`. CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule applies throughout: no success toasts on drop or materialize. Default assumptions to preserve unless the user overrides: split threshold (images 25 MB / videos 100 MB), user-scoped handles, inner zones win over chip, per-tile broken-handle state, thumbnails always uploaded at drop time, handles kept after materialization. If the user picks a different threshold, it's a one-file change in `dropToGenerationConfig.ts`. When searching for the URL-resolution chokepoint in T14, start by grepping `primary_variant?.location` and `item.location` \u2014 wire the resolver in a single place to avoid drift. When identifying entry points in T16, remember there are TWO client-side shot-attach mutations (`useAddImageToShot` and the `WithoutPosition` peer) \u2014 both need gates. Plan validation: all 22 approved plan steps map 1:1 to T1-T21 with T22 as the cross-cutting final verification. Step 16 (identify entry points) is folded into T16 per the 'no inspect tasks' rule \u2014 the identification work is the first step of T16's implementation.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Drop-to-Generation feature. When user drags an image or video file anywhere over the app, show a new small drop zone above the GenerationsPane's control tab (the pane handle). Dropping there uploads the file as a new generation. For files >=50MB, keep the file local via FileSystemHandle persisted in IndexedDB, and only upload when a worker or timeline agent actually needs the bytes — the gallery displays the local file via blob URL in the meantime. Milestones: (1) Global drag detection + chip above pane tab (UI only, no drop action yet). (2) <50MB path — drop uploads via existing image/video uploaders + creates a generation with no shot attachment. (3) >=50MB path — FileSystemHandle + IndexedDB persistence, local-mode generation rows, thumbnail-only upload. (4) Media resolution — gallery displays local-mode generations via blob URL, with permission re-grant UX. (5) Lazy upload at task-build time for local-mode generations, with storage_mode flip. (6) Fallback path for browsers without FileSystemHandle API (Firefox/Safari force upload with warning). Research context already gathered — see arguments passed to megaplan-skill for concrete paths/lines (GenerationsPane.tsx, PaneControlTab.tsx, panesStore.ts, externalImageDrop.ts, imageUploader.ts, videoUploader.ts, storagePaths.ts, MediaGallery/types.ts). Key design decisions to flag as questions: (a) split threshold images 25MB / videos 100MB vs unified 50MB; (b) project-scoped vs user-scoped local handles; (c) precedence vs inner tool drop zones (stopPropagation); (d) handle invalidation when source file moves/renames; (e) whether to always upload small downsized thumbnails for local-mode gallery display without handle permission. Risks to call out: FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle (prompt state across reloads), Chromium-only API coverage, blob URL lifetime in React, lazy-upload failure modes mid-task-build, concurrent worker consumption of same local handle.

    "Document-level `preventDefault` on dragover/drop (Files types only) MUST remain additive to feature-local drop zones (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts:431-508`, `src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useClipManager.ts:377-417`, shot-group droppables). Inner zones attach element-scoped listeners; both fire via bubbling \u2014 do not stopPropagation globally.",
    "FLAG-001: Both `.not('location', 'is', null)` filters in `useProjectGenerations.ts:264-268` AND :318-319 must be updated identically; otherwise count/data queries drift and pagination offsets skew.",
    "FLAG-002: Without the document-level preventDefault, a user dropping a file that misses every zone causes the browser to navigate away from the app. This must ship in Phase 1 before any local-handle work.",
    "FLAG-003: Client-side gates alone are insufficient \u2014 the server-side timeline agent path (`supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts:371-405`, `tools/generation.ts:384-411`) cannot see IndexedDB. Both the shot-attach pre-materialization AND the edge-fn guard must land.",
    "FileSystemHandle permission lifecycle: `queryPermission` only returns `granted|denied|prompt`; `requestPermission` MUST be called inside a user gesture. The resolver must never auto-request \u2014 always surface `needs-permission` and let the overlay trigger on click.",
    "Blob URL lifetime: register once per generation id in a module-level map, revoke on unmount or id change. Multiple gallery tiles sharing a generation must share the same URL or you leak.",
    "Single-flight in `materializeLocalGeneration` is load-bearing \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. Use a module-level `Map<generationId, Promise>`.",
    "Materialization failure modes (permission-denied, handle-missing, network) must each distinctly flip the row back to `local` and surface an actionable error code \u2014 never leave a row stuck in `storage_mode='uploading'`.",
    "Split threshold (25 MB images / 100 MB videos) is assumed. Export from `dropToGenerationConfig.ts` so it's a single-file tune knob. If the user picks unified 50 MB, this is still one-file change.",
    "Inner tool drop zones must continue working after the global listener lands \u2014 manually verify video-editor, join-clips, and shot-group drops in Phase 1 validation (T4).",
    "Generation transformer change relaxes `RawGeneration.location` to `string | null`. Every downstream consumer that indexes `.location` without null checks is a latent crash \u2014 grep `primary_variant?.location || item.location` for all call sites and audit them.",
    "Worker/edge pipeline must keep working for existing remote-mode generations. The migration is additive (default `'remote'`) but Phase 4 transformer/query changes must not regress remote-row rendering.",
    "After successful materialization, keep the IndexedDB handle AND the `local_media_handles` row per the approved assumption \u2014 zero disk cost, supports retry if remote copy is later lost.",
    "Video thumbnail helper: the plan mentions a ~30 LOC first-frame canvas capture if no existing helper is found. Before writing one, search `src/shared/media/` for existing helpers (`extractVideoPosterFrame`, `extractAndUploadThumbnailOnly`, `videoThumbnailGenerator.ts`) \u2014 reuse beats reimplementing.",
    "CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule: NO success toast on drop \u2014 only surface toasts for unsupported MIME, oversized-in-Phase-2, upload failure, and materialization failure.",
    "Edge fn deploy discipline: deploy `create-task` individually (`npx supabase functions deploy create-task --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc`) \u2014 never batch.",
    "DB migration discipline: `npx supabase db push --linked` \u2014 NEVER `db reset --linked`.",
    "Shot-attach pre-materialization makes 'attach a large local generation to a shot' a slow operation. The approved UX is a progress toast \u2014 do not silently hang; show per-file upload progress.",
    "Cross-browser fallback (Firefox/Safari) should gracefully degrade to full upload, never leave the user with a half-inserted generation or a crashed drop handler.",
    "Gallery list-view MUST use `thumbnail_url` (uploaded at drop time) \u2014 only the lightbox should open the local handle. This is how we avoid a permission prompt on every page load."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: Phase 1 (T1-T4) MUST ship before any drop logic because the document-level `preventDefault` protects the rest of the feature from the 'drop misses every zone \u2192 browser navigates away' hazard. Phases 2\u21923\u21924\u21925\u21926 are sequential by data dependency (migration before query, query before resolver, resolver before materialization). Cross-phase: never let a commit land with typecheck failing \u2014 run `npm run typecheck` after each phase and after T13 in particular, since relaxing `RawGeneration.location` will surface latent null-unsafe consumers. The two highest-risk areas are (a) blob URL lifetime \u2014 memoize in a module-level registry keyed by generation id and revoke on unmount, and (b) `materializeLocalGeneration` single-flight \u2014 concurrent task-build + shot-attach for the same local generation must upload exactly once. For the edge fn (T18), deploy individually per CLAUDE.md \u2014 never batch. For the migration (T8), `db push --linked` only, never `db reset`. CLAUDE.md 'errors only' toast rule applies throughout: no success toasts on drop or materialize. Default assumptions to preserve unless the user overrides: split threshold (images 25 MB / videos 100 MB), user-scoped handles, inner zones win over chip, per-tile broken-handle state, thumbnails always uploaded at drop time, handles kept after materialization. If the user picks a different threshold, it's a one-file change in `dropToGenerationConfig.ts`. When searching for the URL-resolution chokepoint in T14, start by grepping `primary_variant?.location` and `item.location` \u2014 wire the resolver in a single place to avoid drift. When identifying entry points in T16, remember there are TWO client-side shot-attach mutations (`useAddImageToShot` and the `WithoutPosition` peer) \u2014 both need gates. Plan validation: all 22 approved plan steps map 1:1 to T1-T21 with T22 as the cross-cutting final verification. Step 16 (identify entry points) is folded into T16 per the 'no inspect tasks' rule \u2014 the identification work is the first step of T16's implementation.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
